250.248.6249
Heart Haven RV (formerly Big Tent RV)  is located right beside the Orange Bridge in the south end of Parksville, BC - in the heart of Vancouver Island’s Oceanside area.
We’re only minutes away from restaurants and shopping in  Parksville , the wonderful Parksville beach, golfing, hiking, mountain bike trails, horseback riding, and kayaking.
Englishman River runs through our backyard and many of our sites have a river view. Learn more about the features and amenities of our facilities by clicking the link below.
Please note that our RV Park caters specifically to recreational vehicles only and for this reason we are unable to accommodate tent camping or car/vehicle camping within our park.
Facility Features
Photo Tour
Map & Directions
We are located at the south edge of Parksville, on Island Highway E at the Englishman River bridge – find map & driving directions here.
Events Calendar
There are over 140 annual events and festivals in the Parksville and Qualicum Beach region. Explore some here.
Nearby Attractions
Check out some of the attractions and activities the area has to offer, from golfing to beautiful beaches, kayaking, and more.

250.248.6249
Heart Haven RV (formerly Big Tent RV)  is located right beside the Orange Bridge in the south end of Parksville, BC - in the heart of Vancouver Island’s Oceanside area.
We’re only minutes away from restaurants and shopping in  Parksville , the wonderful Parksville beach, golfing, hiking, mountain bike trails, horseback riding, and kayaking.
Englishman River runs through our backyard and many of our sites have a river view. Learn more about the features and amenities of our facilities by clicking the link below.
Please note that our RV Park caters specifically to recreational vehicles only and for this reason we are unable to accommodate tent camping or car/vehicle camping within our park.
Facility Features
Photo Tour
Map & Directions
We are located at the south edge of Parksville, on Island Highway E at the Englishman River bridge – find map & driving directions here.
Events Calendar
There are over 140 annual events and festivals in the Parksville and Qualicum Beach region. Explore some here.
Nearby Attractions
Check out some of the attractions and activities the area has to offer, from golfing to beautiful beaches, kayaking, and more.

Facilities
Home
Facilities
We're OPEN for long term RV stays year round!
Heart Haven features over 50 long-term RV sites, all fully serviced with 30amp power. Refer to the  park map  for further details on site amenities. Enjoy swimming in the nearby Englishman River, and catch & release fly fishing.
Please note that our RV Park caters specifically to recreational vehicles only and for this reason we are unable to accommodate tent camping or car/vehicle camping within our park.
250.248.6249     Please call for more info for booking your stay!
Fully Serviced Long-term RV Sites

Water and sewer hook-ups
Cable hook-ups
Amenities
Coin-operated showers
Coin-operated laundry
Pets permitted on leash
Basketball hoop
Sand play area
Catch and release fly fishing
Swimming in the river

### https://www.hearthavenrvcamping.ca/parkville-rv-park-rules
Rules at Heart Haven RV in Parksville
Park Map
Facilities
Photo Tour
Rules
Parksville Guide
Events Calendar

Please call for more info for booking your stay!
250.248.6249
Rules
Home
Rules
Deposits are  NON-REFUNDABLE
Absolutely  NO SUBLETTING  your unit or site.
You are responsible for your visitors and you must be present for them to stay. Maximum 2 weeks.
NO TARPS ..only short term for an emergency and 1 week maximum and ok'd by management.
No unregistered vehicles on property. Vehicles must be in use (no storage insurance), no vehicle repairs, no washing vehicles.
Rent is due on the first of each month.  NO EXCEPTIONS . A late fee of $25.00 will apply.
No garbage stored at sites to help ensure no rodent infestation. Limit of two black bags per week per site. Only household garbage.... No furniture, electronics, carpets, etc. Cardboard -flattened and placed in silver trailer by garage.  NO CARDBOARD  in garbage.
Pets must be leashed  AT ALL TIMES .
Anything outside of your unit, on your site must be in use, clean and in good repair.  ABSOLUTELY NO STORAGE . If you need storage, please check out the storage facilities in town.
Any improvements ie: sheds, stairs, decks,etc. must be approved by management before purchasing and assembling/building.
ABSOLUTELY NO SMOKING  in washroom and laundry room.
Quiet time 10pm-8am
One months' notice to vacate in writing.
Last month's rent for deposit is non-refundable and will be held until you have given one months' notice to vacate your site.
No fires in upper RV Park ... fires permitted in lower RV Park where designated.
2 bags of household garbage per week.
Violation of the park rules may lead to eviction .Thank you for your cooperation and care.
The Management at Heart Haven RV Park
Home
